- **cloverleaf-approve-baselines**: Human baseline-approval gate for the Cloverleaf delivery council. This skill is clear-only — it presents the new baseline images, records human approval, and clears baselines_pending (it drives no FSM transition). Re-run /cloverleaf-run &lt;TASK-ID&gt; afterwards so the delivery council can pass. Usage — /cloverleaf-approve-baselines &lt;TASK-ID&gt;. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cluster-specificity-curve-analysis))
- **cluster-stability-quantification**: Use when clustering transcriptomic (bulk or single-cell) data with an unknown number of clusters and you must algorithmically determine the optimal k and decide when to stop recursive subdivision. - **cluster-stability-scoring**: Use when multiple conflicting cell-cluster annotations (e.g. Leiden at resolutions 1/2/3, Seurat3, Monocle3, ICGS2, cellHarmony, Azimuth label transfer) exist for the same cells and each cluster's stability must be scored before reconciliation. - **cluster-stale-cleanup**: Use to find and clean stale K8s resources in homelab — failed/evicted pods, completed Jobs lacking ttlSecondsAfterFinished, zero-replica ReplicaSets beyond revisionHistoryLimit, unbound PVCs, released PVs, stuck Helm releases, orphan ConfigMaps. Outputs scan table + GitOps cleanup recommendations. NEVER raw `kubectl delete` for git-managed objects — patches manifests (ttlSecondsAfterFinished, revisionHistoryLimit) then commits via /gitops-workflow. Released PVs need cleanup outside GitOps. Fix stuck Helm releases in Git. ([AKhozya/homelab](https://github.com/AKhozya/homelab/tree/HEAD/agents/skills/cluster-stale-cleanup))

Resolution selection, cluster quality assessment, and cell state scoring. Use when the user asks about clustering, finding cell types, annotating clusters, identifying marker genes, or visualizing clusters on UMAP. Works on any normalized AnnData with a computed neighbor graph — AVITI24, scRNA-seq, or AnnData built from a CSV/parquet count matrix. ([Elembio/agent-skills](https://github.com/Elembio/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/multiomics/skills/clustering-and-annotation))
- **clustering-and-persona-discovery**: Use when discovering customer, account or behavioural segments from data rather than defining them by rule — persona discovery, product-usage segmentation, or finding structure in a customer base. Chooses the algorithm from the data shape, validates that clusters are stable rather than an artefact of initialisation, and tests whether the segments actually differ on outcomes that matter. Prevents a beautifully-named segmentation that reshuffles next month and predicts nothing. ([chimeaholu/agentic-gtm-analytics-skillpack](https://github.com/chimeaholu/agentic-gtm-analytics-skillpack/tree/HEAD/ml/clustering-and-persona-discovery) · ★1)
- **clustering-and-segmentation**: Group customers, products, stores, sessions or any records into segments, and pick the clustering method that fits the data and the business use. Use whenever someone wants to segment, cluster or group similar items, build personas or cohorts from behaviour, run k-means, hierarchical clustering, DBSCAN or Gaussian mixtures, choose the number of clusters, read an elbow plot, silhouette score or Calinski-Harabasz index, profile or name clusters, build an RFM segmentation, cluster mixed categorical and numeric data, or check whether segments someone else built are real. - **clustering-ari-agreement-evaluation**: Use when an ICAT run (control Louvain → NCFS upweighting → semi-supervised Louvain) has produced `sslouvain` assignments and you must quantify recovery against simulated ground-truth `Population` labels via adjusted Rand index. - **clustering-ari-evaluation**: Use when comparing integrative or single-dataset clustering results against simulated or known ground-truth labels, e.g. judging whether KLIC's kernel-weighted clustering outperforms COCA, iCluster, or single consensus matrices by ARI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/clustering-ari-evaluation))
- **clustering-ari-evaluation**: Use when comparing clustering-based cell type identification (Seurat, SC3) against a supervised annotator on scRNA-seq data with known true labels, and you need ARI as the comparable metric across training/test size conditions. - **clustering-benchmark-across-datasets**: Use when you need to demonstrate that an unsupervised clustering method yields consistent cluster counts and concordant assignments across repeated runs and across multiple bulk or single-cell transcriptome datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-benchmark-across-datasets))
- **clustering-benchmark-against-author-annotations**: Use when evaluating a proposed clustering/annotation strategy against an author-curated clustering on a published scRNA-seq dataset (e.g. reproducing a paper's resolution-selection criterion on a reference atlas with known sub_cluster labels). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-benchmark-against-author-annotations))
- **clustering-benchmark-comparison**: Use when a single-cell RNA-seq clustering method must be statistically compared against established baselines (e.g., Seurat, SC3, SIMLR) over a multi-dataset benchmark using ARI significance testing and embedding-quality metrics such as ASW. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-benchmark-comparison))
- **clustering-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when a new dimension reduction/clustering method for paired spatial mRNA+ATAC data must be compared to competitors (chromVAR, cisTopic, LSA, PeakVI, SCALE, scMDC, Seurat, SpatialPCA) using label agreement and representation quality metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/clustering-benchmark-evaluation))
- **clustering-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when a clustering method must be validated against baselines on datasets with known ground-truth labels (Splatter-simulated scRNA-seq, synthetic ring/half-moon/Gaussian-mixture shapes), and purity/ARI plus paired significance testing are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-benchmark-evaluation--81d44d2437fbd392))
- **clustering-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when a new single-cell clustering or batch-correction method must be compared quantitatively against published baselines on multiple real scRNA-seq datasets with known cell-type labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-benchmark-evaluation--b5a59eacf66c894b))
- **clustering-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when a new clustering method must be rigorously compared to competitors (e.g., PhenoGraph, HDBSCAN, Louvain/Leiden implementations) on simulated or biological datasets where ground-truth labels are available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-benchmark-evaluation--1718719c4ac2b33a))
- **clustering-benchmark-metric-computation**: Use when comparing clustering algorithms on labeled FASTA datasets with TSV ground-truth files, to produce a per-method × per-dataset table of intrinsic and external clustering evaluation metrics. - **clustering-benchmark-metric-implementation**: Use when you have cluster info TSVs (filename, scan, mass, rt_time, cluster) from msCluster/Falcon/MaRaCluster and need label-free purity, N10, or percent-clustered metrics to compare clustering tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/clustering-benchmark-metric-implementation))
- **clustering-benchmark-reproduction**: Use when reproducing or validating the SSRE NMI/ARI clustering benchmark on Splatter-simulated scRNA-seq data, or when comparing a single-cell clustering method against published Tables S1-S2 scores. - **clustering-driven-memory-compression-on-device**: Compress user-specific memories for LLM personalization by clustering semantically similar memories and merging within clusters, reducing token count while preserving generation quality. Based on Bohdal et al. (ICASSP 2026). - **clustering-dtw**: Cluster regular/smooth time series by SHAPE using Dynamic Time Warping (DTW) as a drop-in alternative to the Euclidean K-Means in notebook 04 (04 Clustering). USE FOR: DTW clustering, dynamic time warping, cluster time series by shape, shape-based clustering, phase-shift / time-shift tolerant clustering, elastic distance clustering, tslearn TimeSeriesKMeans, DTW silhouette, group series with similar patterns regardless of timing, alternative clustering method for notebook 04, replace K-Means with DTW, soft-DTW clustering, DTW barycenter averaging, Sakoe-Chiba band. Follows the notebook-04 pip… ([mmbellani/time-series-forecasting-accelerator-agent](https://github.com/mmbellani/time-series-forecasting-accelerator-agent/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/clustering-dtw) · ★1)

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/clustering-metric-benchmarking))
- **clustering-metric-computation**: Use when comparing cell-cluster assignments produced by two or more scRNA-seq clustering methods against ground-truth cell-type labels and need ARI, NMI and V-measure scores to rank methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-metric-computation))
- **clustering-metric-computation-ari-asw-kbet**: Use when comparing batch-integration methods on annotated scRNA-seq AnnData with 'batch_indices' and 'cell_types' columns and you need quantitative ARI/ASW/kBET scores to judge biology preservation vs. batch over-correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-metric-computation-ari-asw-kbet))
- **clustering-metric-computation-ari-lisi-db**: Use when comparing cell-state clustering quality of methods (e.g., ICAT vs Seurat/Scanorama/Harmony integration) on scRNA-seq perturbation datasets that have ground-truth cell labels and treatment conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-metric-computation-ari-lisi-db))
- **clustering-metric-computation-ari-nmi-purity-homogeneity**: Use when comparing integration methods (e.g., SSGATE, MOFA+, Seurat v5, totalVI, scMM) on paired transcriptome-proteome single-cell data via Leiden clustering, or when reproducing noise-robustness rankings under degraded data quality. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/clustering-metric-computation-ari-nmi-purity-homogeneity))
- **clustering-metric-computation-ari-nmi-vmeasure**: Use when comparing clustering assignments of scRNA-seq cells against ground-truth labels (e.g., FACS-sorted CD4+/CD8+ T cells or Tabula Muris Senis cell types) and reporting ARI, NMI, and V-measure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-metric-computation-ari-nmi-vmeasure))
- **clustering-metric-computation-nmi-ari-asw**: Use when evaluating predicted single-cell cluster assignments against ground-truth cell type labels, or comparing learned cell embeddings of scMGCA vs baselines (scziDesk, scDeepCluster, DCA, DEC, DESC, scVI, scCAEs, scGNN, scGAE, GraphSCC, Seurat, SHARP). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-metric-computation-nmi-ari-asw))
- **clustering-metric-computation-vs-true-labels**: Use when scRNA-seq cell embeddings have been produced by scGCL or one of its ablation variants (task=clustering, epochs=300) and you need agreement metrics against ground-truth cell labels to compare variants across datasets like Adam and Romanov. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/clustering-quality-metric-computation))
- **clustering-quality-metric-computation**: Use when scoring simulated scRNA-seq datasets that carry ground-truth cell-group labels against clustering-quality metrics (ASW, Dunn, Connectivity, DB, CDI) to rank simulation methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/clustering-quality-metric-computation))
- **clustering-quality-metric-computation**: Use when comparing two or more cell-by-embedding matrices of the same single-cell dataset against identical cell-type labels — e.g., benchmarking a 137-GEP dimension reduction against PCA, scDHA, or scMGCA embeddings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-quality-metric-computation))
- **clustering-quality-metric-evaluation**: Use when you have run SCA on a single-cell counts matrix (e.g., setA PBMC clusters 1-5) and need to judge which clusters the autoencoder encoding reliably reconstructs, especially clusters whose QCM/QCC scores sit near the 0.5 significance threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-quality-metric-evaluation))
- **clustering-quality-metric-tabulation**: Decision support for choosing a spectrum clustering tool when you have cluster assignments from multiple tools plus peptide-labeled PSMs (e.g., 1% FDR) and need to compare cluster size distributions, purity, completeness, and data-volume reduction at a comparable error rate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/clustering-quality-metric-tabulation))
